- **conclave-bug**: Report a bug (post-merge regression) or list the open bug backlog. `report` runs a Haiku pre-analysis on the raw input, asks targeted questions only when ambiguity would change the fix direction, and produces a BUG-NNN artifact with Gherkin repro steps and a suspected code area. When `lab_test.enabled` in config, the Tech Lead also generates an executable e2e lab spec (BUG-NNN-lab.md). Mirrors the bug as a GitHub issue. QA does NOT approve the PR — that is the Tech Lead's call via /conclave-pr-review.
